營收走勢圖

研究員/莊佳蓉 EVA8894122@uni-psg.com

研究部維持台達電買進投資建議，主係：(1) 3Q26受缺料影響出貨
遞延，並認列存貨跌價損失，但漲價效益將自4Q26起逐步反映，延
續至1Q27，毛利率可望逐步回升，營運最壞情況已過；(2)資料中
心相關營收占比已達五成，液冷今年占比約13%，全年營收可望突
破900億元，明年仍維持成長；(3) 缺電與缺料雖然影響資料中心
建置節奏，但管理層表示訂單能見度仍高，主要是出貨時點遞延，
需求並未消失；(4) 股價回檔幅度已大，評價已明顯回落。綜上所
述，研究部微幅下修27年營收1.04兆元(原估1.05兆元)，EPS下修
至60.37元(原估62.68元)。評價方面，考量HVDC滲透率仍不明朗，
僅給予31倍本益比，目標價由2,400元下修至1,870元。整體而言，
營運最壞情況已過且股價回檔已多，維持買進建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26受缺料干擾，毛利率承壓但OPM大致持穩 
3Q26營收1,971億元(原估2,102億元)， 季增7.5%， 年增31.1%，
營收達成率93.7%，低於預期，主因是資料中心客戶長短料、
建置延遲，造成出貨遞延。毛利率降至34.1%(原估35.4%)，季
減1.6pts， 低於預期 ， 主係因出貨遞延公司因此認列約39億元
存貨跌價損失(約影響1.7%，涉及產品非VR相關產品，研究部
推測為ASIC產品)，加上漲價在3Q26幾乎還沒反映。不過費用
控管得宜，營利率16.6%(原估16.9%)，僅季減0.1pts，本業獲
利能力大致持穩 。 業外收益受股市評價影響 ， 由2Q26的44億元
降至10億元，EPS為9.04元(原估10.54元)，低於預期。

2. 4Q26毛利率可望止跌回升，營運低點已過 
由於缺料尚未完全解除，研究部微幅調整4Q26營收至2,248億
元(原估2,253億元)，但季增14.1%、年增39.1%，成長動能仍
強。獲利方面，公司表示4Q毛利率優於3Q，主係存貨跌價損失
新增認列有望減少 、 開始有較多新價格體現， 以及電源業務成
長 帶 動 產 品 組 合 改 善 ， 故 研 究 部 預 估 毛 利 率34.8%( 原估
35.8%)， 季增0.7pts； 營利率17.2%(原估18.2%)， 季增0.6pts；
EPS下修至10.14元(原估11.03元)。展望1Q27，研究部預估營
收2,308億元，季增2.6%，表現優於以往的淡季季節性，主要
是部分AI遞延訂單可望延續至1Q27出貨。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. 資料中心營收占比過半，訂單能見度仍高 
管理層表示 ， 今年資料中心相關營收已占總營收一半 ，冷卻系
統約占13%，液冷3Q營收約250億元，26年營收可望突破900億
元 ， 且公司對1H27液冷需求看法仍偏樂觀 。雖然缺電影響部分
資料中心的建置進度 ，但管理層強調訂單能見度高， 變化主要
是出貨時點的延後，並非需求消失。此外，研究部認為，台達
電在供應鏈管理上具備優勢， 隨著缺料逐漸緩解 ，遞延的需求
可望陸續轉為營收。

4. 然Rubin Ultra機櫃出現低壓方案，HVDC滲透率仍待觀察 
HVDC方面 ， 正負400V進展較快 ， 已開始出貨 ；800V則由NV主推 ，
進度較慢。根據供應鏈調查，Ultra平台目前已有低壓設計方
案，若TDP增幅不大，HVDC的省電效益相對有限，客戶未必非
用800V不可，因此研究部下修2027年營收預估。此外，公司已
通過馬來西亞檳城新據點的投資案 ， 為中長期AI電源需求預先
擴充產能。在Vicor相關訴訟上，仍在ITC調查程序中，短期對
出貨不會有影響，公司對自身IP技術具有信心。

5. 評價已回落，維持買進 
綜上所述，研究部下修2026年營收至7,645億元(原估7,781億
元)，年增37.8%；毛利率35.3%(原估35.9%)；EPS下修至36.77
元(原估39.16元)。 同步下修2027年營收至1.04兆元(原估1.05
兆元)， 年增36.4%； 毛利率36.1%(原估36.5%)；EPS下修至60.37
元(原估62.68元)，年增64.2%。考量HVDC滲透率仍不明朗，僅
給予31倍本益比， 目標價由2,400元下修至1,870元 。 惟股價評
價已明顯回落，且營運最壞情況已過，維持買進建議。

評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

公司簡介 
聯發科為台系 IC 設計龍頭廠商，為全球第
五大 IC 設計業者 (僅次於 Qualcomm、
Broadcom、nVidia)，著墨智慧型手機、智
慧裝置與電源管理晶片的發展。

王文雯 wenwen.wang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

聯發科 (2454 TT) 
ASIC 業務引領營運成長

永豐觀點 
ASIC 業務營收規模優於預期，2028 年還有第二個 ASIC 客戶步入量產(屬於 AI 機
櫃設計等級，公司能承接的工作範圍更多)，帶動營運顯著成長。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：ASIC 業務貢獻優於預期，研究部上修獲利，預估聯發科 2026 年稅後
EPS 72.8 元(原預估 70.5 元)，2027 年稅後 EPS 142.78 元(原預估 132.52 元)，
2028 年稅後 EPS 321.22 元(原預估 312.93 元)。聯發科 ASIC 業務進展優於預期，
可望帶動後續營運強勁成長，研究部對聯發科維持買進，目標價由5570 元調升至
5800 元(由 2027~2028 年 EPS 均值與 25 倍評價)。

ESG 評析 
聯發科企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向
平均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學)

專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用

### Page 2

2

聯發科為 台系 IC 設計龍頭廠商 ：聯發科為全球第五大 IC 設計業者 (僅次於
Qualcomm、Broadcom、nVidia)，著墨智慧型手機、智慧裝置與電源管理晶片的
發展 。 在智慧型手機晶片方面的主要競爭對手為Qualcomm、 展訊及華為海思等 ，
在智慧家庭、AIoT 等應用領域則以 Broadcom、Marvell、聯詠及瑞昱等廠商為
主。客戶主要透過代理商供應產品，終端客戶以全球手機品牌(OPPO、VIVO、小
米、Samsung、榮耀等)以及智慧家電品牌為主。 
3Q26 財報優於預期 ：聯發科 3Q26 營收 1680.58 億元(+10%QoQ，+18%YoY)，
毛利率 46.3%， 營業利益率16.1%， 稅後淨利278.82 億元(+15%QoQ，+11%YoY)，
稅後 EPS 17.37 元，財報優於預期，主要是營收超標、營業費用控管得宜所致。 
聯發科 3Q26 財報各個細項表現如下：(1) 營收季增 10%，超逾目標值 1522 億元
~1598 億元(QoQ+0%~+5%)，三大產品線表現均優於預期，得益於專案挹注程
度與供應鏈產能爭取進度較佳，三大產品線以Smart Edge Platform 挹注最顯著。
聯發科 3Q26 三 大 產 品 線 營 運 動 能 如 下 ：Smart edge platform 季增
20%(+68%YoY， 營收佔比58%)、 電源晶片(+1%QoQ，+18%YoY， 營收佔比5%)、
手機營收滑落 1%QoQ(-17%YoY，營收佔比 37%)，其中 Smart edge platform
季增幅度超逾公司原預期的，QoQ+5%~+9%，公司前次法說即已表示將有多項
聯網與車載項目於 3Q26 進入量產 ， 現在看來5G modem/AI tablet(天璣 9500 導
入三星平板電腦 Tab S120/AI PC / 車載晶片貢獻更優，並且 TV SoC 內含 DRAM
漲價亦有貢獻。 電源晶片亦優於公司原預期的持平， 主要是來自運算與資料中心市
占率提升。手機僅季減1%，落於公司原預期的 QoQ-5%~+0%的上緣，應受惠於
旗艦晶片天璣 9600 出台所致。(2) 毛利率 46.3%，與前季相仿，落於目標值區間
44.5%~47.5%區間。(3) 營業費用 507 億元(+7%QoQ)，營業費用成長性<營收
成長性，反映營業費用控管得宜。(4) 聯發科 3Q26 存貨 1535 億元(+56%QoQ)，
庫存天數由 2Q26 的 102 天增至 127 天，主要是因應 ASIC 業務拓展。

4Q26 營收展望優於預期：聯發科預估 4Q26 營收 2101 億元~2218 億元(以美元
對新台幣匯率 31.7 元計算)，QoQ+25%~+32%，YoY+40% ~+48%，毛利率目
標值區間下滑 1%至 43.5%~46.5%，營業費用率 24%~28%，反映營業利益率
16%~23%，優於過往三年多落於 14%~19%水準。聯發科 4Q26 展望細項如下：
(1) 4Q26 營運由 ASIC 業務驅動，(2) 4Q26 毛利率目標值區間下滑 1%，主要是受
ASIC 業務毛利率<公司均值，以及晶片價格因製造成本上升而出現轉嫁事宜的綜
合因素影響。(3) ASIC 業務的營業利益率>公司均值，故帶動公司 4Q26 營業利益
率回升。(4) 公司預期 2026 年營收成長性，超逾原預期的 YoY+5%~+9%。研究
部預估聯發科 4Q26 營收 2180 億元(＋30%QoQ，+45%YoY)，毛利率 45%，稅
後獲利 405.2 億元(+45%QoQ，+77%YoY)，稅後 EPS 25.25 元。 
ASIC 業務貢獻值優於預期：聯發科本次法說揭示 ASIC 業務貢獻值優於預期：(1) 
公司預期 2026 年 ASIC 業務 25 億美元(優於前次法說僅預期 >20 億美元)，
Zebrafish 已在 09/2026 開始小量出貨。(2) 2027 年 ASIC 業務 160 億美元(市佔
率 20%)，落於公司原預期的市占率 15%~20%與貢獻 120 億~160 億美元的上
緣。(3) 第二款 ASIC Humufish 維持預計將於 1Q28 步入量產，目前該平台部分
die 業已 tape out 完成，主要 die 也在 tape out 進行當中，先進封裝仍將優先採
用 Intel EMIB 。(4) 2027 年的營業利益率將>20%，反映營運槓桿效益提升。(5) 
第三代 ASIC(若順利應於 2029~2030 貢獻)則目前尚未定義最終規格。 
再者，公司亦表示第二個 ASIC 業務(搭載 NVLink Fusion) 預計 2028 年開始出貨
(前季法說僅表示尚與潛在客戶洽談中，本季已明確表示將於 2028 年出貨)，此為
新客戶(公司表示非中國客戶，故應表示該客戶非先前傳言的 Bytedance)。協助單
顆晶片設計與進入 NVLink Fusion 機櫃級設計的差異，在於幫客戶將晶片納入整
合進 Nvidia 的 AI 機櫃系統，需要增加高速互聯/相容性/記憶體架構/封裝協同設
計，以與 Nvidia 的交換器或網路系統偕同運作，反映聯發科能承接的工作範圍更
多，亦即接到單一客戶的商業價值應該更多。但因為此為聯發科首次導入 AI 機櫃
等級的業務，相關客戶與細項內容，均尚難知曉，故研究部本次財務預估尚未將此
估算在內。 
維持買進：考量 ASIC 業務貢獻優於預期，研究部上修獲利，預估聯發科 2026 年
營收 6874 億元(+15%YoY)，毛利率 45.87%，營業利率 16.9%，稅後獲利 1168
億元(+11%YoY)，稅後 EPS 72.8 元(原預估 70.5 元)，2027 年營收 1.17 兆元
(+71%YoY)， 毛利率43.77%， 營業利率21.5%， 稅後獲利2292 億元(+96%YoY)，

### Page 3

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): New AI server racks and Foldable phones to drive 4Q26E

We expect Hon Hai’s monthly revenues to continue sequential growth through Dec 
2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +39% QoQ, driven by: (1) AI data center: The 
next-generation AI server racks and CPO switches ramp-up, with higher ASP and early 
delivery. We expect Hon Hai to be the first to deliver the latest model, around 2-3 
months ahead of peers, driving its Oct and onward revenue growth. Followed by 
GPU-powered AI server racks, the LPU-powered AI server racks should fuel the growth 
in Dec and onward, supporting the YoY increase in 1Q27. As our Global Server TAM 
update shows, we expect the AI server rack global value TAM to grow at 163% CAGR in 
2025-28E to US$1tn, or 63% of the global server value TAM by 2028E. And (2) 
Smartphone: riding on the major brand customer’s latest model launches along with the 
new form factor to attract more end users. As we highlighted in September (report link), 
we reduced our global smartphone volume estimates by 1m/ 8m/ 0.2m units to 1.1bn / 
1.2bn/ 1.2bn in 2026-28E, or -10% / +2%/ +2% YoY on high memory costs; however, we 
remain positive on the potential for global leading brands to outperform the market 
given their strong brand awareness, large procurement power, and customers’ stronger 
consumption power. We remain positive on foldable phones and expect premium 
models to continue to gain market share, which should be positive to Hon Hai. Maintain 
Buy (on CL).

3-month revenues preview: We expect the company’s 4Q26E revenues to be +61% 
YoY/ +39% QoQ to NT$4.2 trillion. 3Q26 delivered strong revenues YoY growth of 47%, 
or 5% / 7% ahead of our / Bloomberg consensus, and we expect to see both YoY and 
MoM growth throughout Oct-Dec, driven by new AI server racks, new smartphone 
models, and CPO switches. Management noted that AI-related products remain on track 
to maintain solid growth momentum through 4Q26, while ICT products continue to 
benefit from typical 2H seasonal tailwinds, which should support overall operational 
performance in line with current consensus expectations.

### Page 4

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 17.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.  
Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): AI servers ramp-up to drive growth ahead; model transition and PC 
shipment slowdown could weigh on 4Q26

Quanta Sep revenues declined by 23% MoM, which we attributed to AI servers model 
transition, while PC entered quarter-end at a strong MoM of +62% MoM. Looking 
forward, we expect monthly revenues to resume sequential growth in Oct to Dec, riding 
on a reducing impact of AI servers model transition as new racks ramp up, along with 
growing general servers on agentic AI, and ASP increase on better specification and high 
raw material costs. We expect 4Q26E revenues to decline by 4% QoQ, considering the 
high base in 3Q26 and the gradual ramp-up of new AI server racks. As we highlighted in 
Oct (Global Server TAM updates), we expect global AI server racks (powered by NVIDIA 
and AMD) shipment at 63k / 110k / 164k in 2026E-28E along with the specification 
upgrade to drive the ASP; we raised leading US CSPs aggregate capex by 4% / 17% / 
22% in 2026E-28E to US$1,397bn by 2028E, reflecting our positive view on AI spending.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$300 is based on a 
10.7x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. Key 
upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers. 
Wiwynn (6669.TW, Buy): New ASIC and GPU AI server racks in expansion

Wiwynn delivered strong Sep revenues, which were 19% ahead of our estimates, 
reflecting strong general server demand from US leading CSPs, and its major customers’ 
new ASIC AI server ramping up. We expect revenue YoY growth to remain strong at mid 
double-digit through Dec 2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +60 YoY / +8% QoQ, 
driven by (1) major customers’ new ASIC AI servers ramping up, across air-cooled and 
liquid-cooled, (2) general servers end demand remaining solid on agentic AI, (3) new 
GPU-powered AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26 across NVIDIA and 
AMD platforms, and (4) potential new customers in ASIC AI servers. As we highlighted in 
Oct (Global Server TAM update), we raised AI servers implied AI chips shipment by 9% / 
19% in 2027 / 28E with ASIC penetration rate increasing to 58% by 2028E, and our US 
internet team forecasts that leading US cloud service providers (CSPs) in aggregate will 
grow their capex budgets by 84%/ 52%/ 13% YoY in 2026-28E, with our US CSP capex 
estimates raised by 4% / 17% / 22% in 2026-28E to US$1,397bn by 2028E, supporting 
our positive view on Wiwynn. Maintain Buy with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$4,000

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$4,000. Our target price is 
based on 20.3x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.  
Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): AI server racks to see strong ramp-up with customer expansion and 
specification upgrades

Wistron’s Sep revenue was up 87% YoY to NT$381bn, or 18% lower than our estimate, 
which we attribute to the impact of shipment timing volatility in higher-ASP AI products. 
We expect sequential revenue growth ahead through Dec 2026, driven by new GPU AI 
server racks ramp-up in 4Q26, which should drive up the ASP , along with strong AI 
spending and expansion of the company’s customer base. The new GPU AI server racks 
have various racks across GPU, CPU, and LPU, supporting ODM growth in coming 
quarters. Sep PC shipment was up 7% MoM but down 32% YoY to 1.5m units, reflecting 
the slow PC demand amid the consumption pull-in under the high memory cost. Despite 
PC shipments growth likely being negative HoH considering the strong pull-in since late 
2025, we expect the strong AI server rack ramp-up to offset the impact, driving 2H26 
revenue up +41% HoH. Maintain Buy with an unchanged TP of NT$291 (12.6x 2027E P/E 
vs. 30.4% NI YoY in 2027-28E).

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Inventec  
We are Neutral-rated on Inventec with a 12-month target price of NT$61.0. Our target 
price is based on 14.2x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

(2) broadband, IoT, and game console entering peak season. We are positive on

Pegatron’s Sep revenue was up 87% YoY / 143% MoM to NT$215bn, or 64% higher than 
our estimate. This reflects a strong ramp-up in AI servers in Sep. Sep PC shipments were 
up c.8% MoM but down 44% YoY to 0.46m, reflecting the weak PC demand under the 
high memory costs. Prior to September, the company suffered from model transition (i.e. 
declining demand for older models while new models were in a slow shipment ramp-up 
phase).

Earnings revision: We factor in Pegatron’s Sep revenue and raise our 2026-28E net 
income by 4% / 6% / 4%, mainly on higher revenues and lower opex ratio. We raise our 
2026-28E revenues by 16% / 28% / 27%, considering the AI server shipment ramp-up 
and higher ASP on better specification. Given the much lower GM of AI server racks 
compared to Pegatron’s other products, our changes in gross profits narrow to 9% / 12% 
/ 15% in 2026E-28E. The opex ratio for 2026-28E is cut by 0.1 ppt/ 0.2 ppt/ 0.1 ppt to 
reflect the improved operational efficiency.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Update
Delta Electronics Inc.  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 GM Dragged by Inventory
Write-off
Despite the secular tailwinds of increasing power consumption
in AI clusters, we think near-term sentiment toward Delta could
remain lackluster post weaker 3Q26 results.
3Q26 earnings fell short on lower GM:
• Off revenue of NT$197,059mn (+8% QoQ/+31% YoY), GM contracted  by
1.6ppt QoQ to 34.1% (vs. MSe/consensus at 35.7%/36.2%), which was
dragged 2ppt lower by the inventory write-off

• Opex was well controlled at NT$34,535mn (opex ratio 17.5%), lower than our
estimate of NT$36,983mn (opex ratio 18.4%) and consensus of NT$37,129mn
(opex ratio 18.0%)
• Operating profit came in at NT$32,620mn (+7% QoQ/+31% YoY; OPM
16.6%), 7% lower  than MSe of NT$34,992mn (OPM 17.4%) and 13% lower
than consensus of NT$37,677mn (OPM 18.2%).
• With non-op gains of NT$1,046mn (including FX gain of NT$415mn), net
profit was NT$23,495mn (-7% QoQ/+26% YoY; EPS NT$9.04), 8%/15% short

of MSe/consensus at NT$25,554mn (EPS NT$9.84)/NT$27,611mn (EPS NT
$10.40).
Our view:
• We believe near-term sentiment toward the stock will stay soft, though the
weak financial performance in 3Q26 was mainly impacted by component
supply tightness
• We believe Delta should grow its revenue by 10% QoQ with margin
expansion in 4Q26, and will continue to benefit from the increasing power
consumption of AI clusters in 2027
• Earnings call at 3pm HKT on Oct 29th  (details here)

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey. Voting
opens 10 November 2026.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Delta Electronics Inc. (2308.TW)
Base case: P/E multiple of 30x based on average 2027-28 EPS.                          We believe our target multiple
is justified by the 63% earnings CAGR we estimate for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.5, versus
the stock's ~23x P/E and 23% earnings CAGR during 2022-25, which implied a PEG of 1.0.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected global economic growth

n Falling raw material prices
n Margin improvement
Risks to Downside
n Global economic slowdown
n Ongoing component supply tightness
n Rising raw material prices
n Margin contraction as a result of higher operating expenses or faster ASP erosion

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

研究員/游周翰 Jeffery.Yu@uni-psg.com

研究部維持聯亞買進投資建議，主要考量：(1)3Q26 營收及獲利皆
優於市場預期；(2)AI Data Center 矽光需求持續強勁，主要客戶
訂單能見度超過 6 個月，4Q26 營收可望續增，1Q27 新增產能開出
將帶動營收明顯跳增；(3)管理層維持先前 2027 年產能擴增指引
(+150%YoY)，並且確立2028 年產能擴增速度與 2027 年相當，亦即
兩年擴增產能超過 6 倍。惟考量(1)2026 年新增後段產能驗證時間
較研究部預期長；(2)3Q26 毛利率及費用率優於預期；(3)2027 年
產能增長幅度優於研究部預期 ， 因此調整2026/2027 年營收分別為
55.2 億元(原估 55.92 億)及 155.6 億元(原估 150.4 億)，EPS 分別
為 20.95 元(原估 19.96 元)及 58.76 元(原估 54.06 元)。目標從
3,800 上修至 4,000 元，重申買進投資建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26營收、EPS均優於市場預期，矽光出貨帶動獲利成長： 
聯亞3Q26營收15.62億元(+27.9%QoQ，+182.6%YoY)，略低研究
部預期，主因為後段產能驗證時間較研究部預期長 ； 營收雙增
主要還是受惠AI Data Center 矽光產品出貨量持續增加。毛
利率58.6%(+1.1pts QoQ，+15 pts YoY)，營業利益為7.71億
元，營益率達49.4%；營業費用1.43億元(+11%QoQ，+4%YoY)，
主因為研發及員工分紅費用增加；稅後淨利 6.21億元，EPS 
6.12元， 優於研究部預期 ， 主因為毛利率及費用控管優於研究
部預估。產品組合方面，Data Center占營收80–85%，其中以
矽光產品為主 ；Telecom占5–10%， 其他約5%， 與上季比重相當。

表一、3Q26 研究部預估與實際差異 
單位：百萬元 實際 研究部原估 差異 
營業收入 1,562 1,596 -2.1% 
營業利益 771 713 +8.1% 
稅後淨利 621 575 +8.0% 
EPS(元) 6.12 5.65 +8.0% 
資料來源： 聯亞財報、統一研究部預估

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. 4Q26營收持續季增，1Q27新增產能帶動出貨加速： 
展望4Q26，主要客戶目前訂單期間已超過6個月，管理層表示
在手訂單金額已超過2026年全年營收規模， 顯現出目前需求
仍 然 十 分 強 勁 。 管 理 層 預 期 營 收 季 增幅 約 與4Q25 相當
(+16.2%QoQ)，即18.15億元 。 然而 ， 依照過往管理層給予的指
引通常較為保守，且在需求強勁以及新加入的 合作夥伴陸續
開出 後 段 產 能 下 ， 研 究 部 預 估 4Q26 營收為 18.32 億元
(+17.3%QoQ，+185.2%YoY)，EPS為6.77元。

展望1Q27，隨相關設備導入及後段委外產能開始量產，管理層
預期1Q27產出量將有更明顯增幅 。研究部預估 1Q27營收為
275.2億元(+50.2%QoQ，+204%YoY)，EPS為10.26。

3. 美國客戶四年約加持，2028年產出目標為2026年的6.25倍： 
聯亞8M26與美系Data Center 客戶簽訂2027–2030年為期四年
的CW Laser供貨長約 ， 客戶並以保證金方式提供資金支持 ， 以
確保未來產品供應 。 為履行長期供貨承諾 ， 公司同步追加近30
億元資本支出計畫，使過去一年累計核准資本支出超過 80億
元 ， 預計2027年產出顆數較2026年增加150%，2028年再較2027
年增加150%， 相當於2028年產出規模達2026年的6.25倍 ， 其中
8月新增資本支出主要貢獻於2028年。此外，為確保擴產後的
原料供應，公司亦與兩家日系及一家美系InP基板供應商簽訂
長期採購合約 ， 並持續增加台灣及海外後段委外代工夥伴 。 研
究部認為，美系客戶透過長期供貨合約及保證金支持聯亞擴
產 ， 顯示客戶對未來CW Laser需求具有高度確定性 ， 亦有助提
高新增產能的去化能見度，支撐公司2027–2028年營運成長。

4. 多波長CW Laser具技術及ASP優勢，NPO較CPO先行： 
聯亞800G及1.6T矽光產品已進入量產，分別具備8顆雷射及8
至16顆不同波長雷射的解決方案。管理層指出，多波長產品
ASP較單波長高出50%以上，且全球具備大規模量產能力的供
應商有限 ， 有利公司維持產品競爭優勢 。 隨未來光通訊傳輸規
格持續升級，公司亦積極開發3.2T及NPO/CPO應用所需的高功
率CW Laser。其中，管理層預期NPO有望於2H27率先作為過渡
方案，目前所接觸客戶以外部光源設計為主；CPO則因光源配
置 、 連接器等相關規格尚未完全定案 ， 大規模導入時程仍有待
觀察。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

此外，隨NPO/CPO等應用對光源功率要求提高，未來單顆 CW 
Laser功率提升至250–300mW以上 。 管理層指出 ， 高功率產品因
晶粒面積增加及良率下降，相同InP基板產能可產出的晶粒顆
數可能減少30–50%，使高功率光源對基板及有效產能的需求
增加 。 研究部認為 ， 短期聯亞仍以既有CW Laser產品擴產及多
波長方案出貨成長為主要動能，中長期則隨 NPO/CPO逐步導
入 ， 高功率光源有望成為新增需求來源 ， 並提高相關產品的製
造及供應門檻，將有望成為其技術護城河，維持競爭優勢。

5. Data Center高速PD完成驗證，預計隨新增產能逐步量產： 
除CW Laser外，聯亞亦持續布局Data Center高速PD產品。管
理層指出 ，200G以上PD已完成客戶驗證 ， 預計2027年新增產能
陸續開出，開始量產 Data Center PD 產品。惟目前仍以 CW 
Laser為主要生產重心，因此PD產出規模相對有限，但有助於
降低產品集中度風險。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

告 中 所 有 的 意 見 及 預 估 ， 如 有 變 更 恕 不 另 行 通 知 。

供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與

陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或

1 群益投顧 
精測 (6510 TT) 前 三 季 大 賺 四 股 本。精 測 受 惠 於 HPC 測 試 需 求 ，3Q26 營 收 、獲 利 皆 創
單季新高， 4Q26 營收有望季增， 2027 年 展 望 正 向 ， 投 資 建 議 維 持Buy 。

研 究 員 ： 莊 禮 誠   
dan.chuang@cim.capital.com.tw

精測 3Q26 獲 利 季 增 、 年 增：  
測試介面大廠精測 10/27 公布 3Q26 營收 19.05 億元，
QoQ+16.17%/YoY+53.35% ；毛 利 率 57.3% ；營 益 率 35 .2 %；稅 後 純 益 5.51 億
元， QoQ+11.61%/YoY+100.03% ；稅後 EPS 16.75 元 。 前 三 季 歸 屬 母 公 司 業
主 合 併 淨 利 13.87 億 元 ， 年 增 逾 90% ， 每 股 純 益 42.2 元 ， 大 賺 逾 四 個 股 本 。 
精測 3Q26 營 收 、 獲 利 皆 創 單 季 新 高：  
精 測 表 示 ，3Q26 營 收 、 獲 利 等 財 務 指 標 均 創 單 季 新 高 ， 並 且 連 續 三 季 賺 逾
一 個 股 本，充 分 展 現 公 司 在 產 品 組 合、成 本 效 率 與 客 戶 需 求 的 競 爭 優 勢 持 續
強 化 ， 為 後 續 成 長 奠 定 穩 固 基 礎 。 
AI 帶 動 晶 片 朝 向高 效 能 、 高 頻 寬 與 高 功 率 架 構 發 展， 精 測 產 品 線 完 整：  
精 測 指 出 ， 隨 著 AI 應 用 全 面 擴 散 ， 推 動 晶 片 朝 高 效 能 、 高 頻 寬 與 高 功 率 架
構 發 展，測 試 複 雜 度 與 技 術 門 檻 同 步 提 高，客 戶 對 高 階 測 試 介 面 的 需 求 日 益
迫 切 ， 該 公 司 已 推 出 AI 與 高 效 能 運 算 （HPC ） 完 整 測 試 介 面 產 品 ， 涵 蓋 晶
圓 測 試 探 針 卡、高 功 率 老 化 測 試 板、IC 測 試 載 板 及 系 統 級 測 試 板 解 決 方 案，
以 滿 足 客 戶 一 次 購 足 的 需 求 。 
精測 完 整 導 入 智 慧 製 造 ， 在 產 能 擴 張 下 ， 有 助 維 持 獲 利 率：  
精 測 強 調 ， 公 司 同 步 精 進 研 發 與 製 造 流 程 ， 導 入AI 智 慧 製 造 ， 以 提 升 製 程
技 術 與 生 產 效 率，強 化 產 品 開 發 時 程 與 品 質 管 理 能 力，使 淨 利 率 在 產 能 擴 張
下 仍 穩 健 維 持 高 檔 水 準 。 
精測 4Q26 營運 審 慎 樂 觀 ：  
精 測 指 出，未 來 將 持 續 依 客 戶 需 求 與 市 場 變 化，審 慎 配 置 資 源，深 化 客 戶 合
作 關 係 。 研 發 重 點 聚 焦 下 一 世 代 探 針 卡 與 測 試 載 板 ， 以 因 應AI 客 製 化 晶 片
需 求 ， 同 時 推 動 「AI in House 」 企 業 智 慧 協 作 ， 探 索 AI 於 研 發 設 計 、 工 程
知 識 管 理 、 供 應 鏈 協 作 及 智 慧 製 造 等 應 用 ， 審 慎 樂 觀4Q26 及 未 來 營 運 。 
投 資 建 議 ：  
精 測 受 惠 於 HPC 測 試 需 求 ， 3Q26 營 收 、 獲 利 皆 創 單 季 新 高， 4Q26 營 收 有
望季增， 2027 年 展 望 正 向 ， 投 資 建 議 維 持 Buy 。

產業速報

產業速報

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

자동차업종

◼ 3Q26 비용절감/환율 효과로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현 
◼ 2027년 물량–환율 영향 상쇄되며 2H27 이익체력 개선 본격화 예상 
◼ 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등에 주목

투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 50만원 하향(-6%) 
현대차 TP 50만원(시총 102조원)은 12MF EPS 41,978원에 Target PER 12배
적용하여 산출. 제한적인 실적 기대 반영해 타겟 밸류 하향함에 따라 TP 조정  
우려 대비 양호한 3Q26 실적으로 주가 하방 부담 완화. 주가의 추세적 상승 
위해서는 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등 확인돼야 할 
것. 기저효과에 따른 물량 개선이 주가 저점 받쳐줄 것이나, 현재 밸류(2027년 
PER 9.1배vs 글로벌 피어 6.1배)에서 추세 상승 이끌긴 어려울 것이기 때문. 
이에, 자동차 본업 외 1) 한/미국 Physical AI 투자 계획 및 2) BD 공급망 구축 
/양산 가시화에 따른 주가 반등 기대

3Q26 매출 45조(YoY -3%, 이하 YoY), OP 2.7조(+6%), OPM 5.9% 시현 
3Q26 OP 추정(2.5조)/컨센(2.4조) 상회. 파업에 따른 생산/판매 차질 영향으로 
기대 제한적이었으나, 비용 절감/환율 영향으로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현. 
[자동차] 매출 33조(-10%), OP 2.1조(+25%), OPM 6.5%. 도매 판매 92.1만대
(-11%). 판매 부진과 원자재 부담에도 불구, 환효과(+1.3조)/비용절감(+0.5조)
로 실적 방어. 이외 YoY 변수별 영향은 물량(-1.1조)/믹스(-0.6조)/원자재(-
0.5조). [금융] 매출 9.2조(+28%), OP 4,710억(-18%), OPM 5.1%, [기타] 매출 
2.9조(+2%), OP 712억(-72%), OPM 2.5% 기록

2027년 매출 191조(YoY +2%, 이하 YoY), OP 12.7조(+15%), OPM 6.6% 전망 
기저효과 바탕으로 연결 도매 물량 YoY +5% 증가 예상하나, 원달러 환율 -
4% 하락(가정 환율 1,380원)하며 1H27 → 2H27로가며 이익체력 개선 예상

### Page 5

DAISHIN SECURITIES

5  
  현대차 3Q26 실적 컨퍼런스콜 QnA 
Q. 금융부문 환율 변동에 따른 4Q26 매출 및 손익 영향 
- 금융부문 환율 영향은 현대차 실적에 미치는 환율 영향과 동일한 방향으로 발생

Q. 3Q26 금융부문 수익성 악화 요인 
- 장기금리 상승에 따른 조달 및 이자비용 증가가 주요인. 자산/부채 만기 매칭에 따른 
금리 상승 영향 반영 
- EV 중심 중고차 가격지수 소폭 하락 및 차량 반환에 따른 매각손실, 금리 상승에 따
른 연체율 및 신용비용 상승 
- 차량 판매가격 인하 영향은 없으며, 3Q26 도매 판매 감소에 따른 금융 영업수익 영향 
불가피했음

Q. 향후 주주환원 정책에서 배당 및 자사주 매입 우선순위 
- 기존 밸류업 정책: 최소 DPS 1만원/TPR 35% 제시. 연간 배당 약 2.6조원 → TPR 
35% 충족 위한 잔여분 자사주 매입/소각 활용 
- 경영환경 변화 고려하여 기존 밸류업 프로그램 전반의 주주가치 제고 방안 재검토중 
- 새로운 정책 확정 및 주주 소통 전까지 기존 약속인 최소 DPS 1만원 유지할 계획 
- 시장의 배당 및 자사주 매입 간 균형 요구를 인지하고 있으며, 주주 의견 수렴 후 주
주가치 제고 계획 공유 예정

Q. 3Q26 영업이익 증감 요인 중 기타 +6,400억 발생 원인 
- 월별 컨틴전시 활동을 통한 비용 최적화 및 재료비 절감 지속 추진 
- 4Q26 원가 절감 효과는 3Q26 대비 확대 예상

Q. 3Q26 영업외손익 감소 원인 
- 3Q26 환율 급락으로 외화 매출채권 등 외화자산 관련 외화환산손실 및 외환거래손실 
발생 
- 매출채권 인식 시점과 회수 시점 간 환율 차이에 따른 외환차손 발생 
- 지분법 대상 기업들의 3Q26 실적 부진에 따른 지분법손익 감소도 일부 영향. 대부분 
외환 관련 손실에서 발생

Q. 중국 OEM 국내 진출 확대에 따른 대응 및 신차/가격 전략 
- 중국 OEM 및 테슬라의 국내 M/S 확대 대응중. 다만, 잦은 가격 조정은 중고차 잔존
가치 및 고객가치 훼손할 수 있어 지양 
- 현대캐피탈의 캡티브 금융 경쟁력 활용. 잔존가치 유예할부 등을 통해 월 납입금을 낮
춰 중국 OEM 대비 경쟁력 확보 
- 서비스 네트워크 기반으로 경쟁사 대비 경쟁력 확보 노력 
- 국내 신차: 4Q26 아반떼 HEV, 투싼, GV90, G80 HEV, 싼타페 FL(2026.12월), G90 
FL 등 출시 예정 
- 북미: 4Q26 투싼, GV80 HEV 출시 및 27년 초 GV90 출시 예상 
- 유럽: 아이오닉3 판매 개시, 4Q26 투싼/베이온 등 주요 모델 출시 예정 
- 중국: 아이오닉 V / 인도: 베이온, 아이오닉 P 등 출시 예정 
- 신차 출시 계획 연도별로 다수 예정. 가격은 원가/경쟁사/소비자 가격 수용성 등을 고
려해 결정

Q. 3Q26 법인세비용 증가 원인 및 4Q26 전망 
- 일회성 요인보다는 법인별 손익 믹스 및 적용 세율 차이에 따른 영향 
- 4Q26 법인세 수준은 QoQ 큰 변동 없을 것으로 전망

- Overweight(비중확대)

  : 향후 6개월간 업종지수상승률이 시장수익률과 유사한 수준 예상

Document 234: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로긴 호흡으로 - 기업

### Page 1

(005930) 
삼성전자 
2026.09.29

반도체 손인준 
6154 / sij@eugenefn.com

긴 호흡으로 
 투자의견 STRONG BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 560,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 270,000원(9/28)

 삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익이 103.6조원(+16%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 메모리 가격 등 전반적인 반도체 업황에 대한 기존 가정은 대체로 
유지했으나, 최근 원화 강세에 따라 원/달러 환율 가정을 1,440원에서 
1,400원으로 낮추고 MX 사업부의 적자 확대를 반영해 실적 추정치를 하
향 조정했다. 또한 2Q26 재고가 3Q26 매출로 인식되는 과정에서 발생하
는 추가 성과급 충당금 부담도 반영했다.  
 DS 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 104.0조원(+17%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 서버 DRAM과 eSSD 중심의 가격 인상이 이어지며, DRAM과 
NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +17%, +18%QoQ 상승할 것으로 추정한
다. 1cnm 수율 개선과 HBM4의 빠른 램프업도 실적 개선에 기여할 전망이
다. Foundry/LSI는 HBM4 베이스다이 생산 확대, Groq 3 LPU 양산 및 선
단 공정 가격 인상에 힘입어 하반기 매출 성장이 예상된다. 다만 성과급 충
당금과 감가상각비 부담으로 영업적자 축소 폭은 제한적일 것으로 보인다. 
 DX 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 -1.8조원(적자지속qoq)을 기록할 것으로 추
정한다. 삼성전자는 메모리 가격 급등에도 제품 가격 인상을 제한하며 시장 
점유율 확대에 주력하고 있다. 이러한 가격 정책과 갤럭시 Z 폴드8 등 신제
품 흥행에 힘입어 판매량은 당초 예상치를 상회할 것으로 보인다. 다만 원
가 상승분의 전가가 제한되면서 영업적자 폭은 크게 확대될 전망이다.  
 삼성전자의 4Q26 영업이익은 114.9조원(+11%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. DRAM과 NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +11%, +12%QoQ 상승
하며 견조한 흐름을 이어갈 것으로 보인다. 환율 가정 조정에 따라 2026년
과 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 각각 365.2조원(+735%yoy), 595.8조원
(+63%YoY)으로 낮췄다. 4Q26~1Q27 범용 DRAM·NAND 및 2027년 
HBM 가격 협상 환경은 공급업체에 유리하게 전개되고 있어, 향후 가격 가
정의 추가 상향 가능성이 높다고 판단한다.  
 극심한 DRAM 공급 부족으로 인한 HBM 적층 단수 하향 조정(base die 수
요 증가), Memory Wall에 대응하기 위한 3D Stacked DRAM·zHBM 도입 
논의 등은 파운드리의 기술적 가치를 부각시키는 요인이다. 파운드리의 수
익성 회복 속도는 완만할 전망이지만, 메모리와 파운드리를 연계한 턴키 비
즈니스의 시너지는 AI 반도체 시장 내 경쟁력을 높이고, 메모리 시장 점유
율 확대에도 기여할 것으로 보인다. 적극적인 메모리 가격 인상 정책으로 
업계 최고 수준의 Blended ASP도 장기간 유지할 것으로 예상한다. 
 이제 메모리 업종 주가는 업황의 지속 가능성을 확인하고 꾸준한 주주환원
을 반영하며, 중장기적으로 점진적인 상승세를 이어갈 것으로 판단한다. 삼
성전자는 메모리·파운드리를 아우르는 턴키 역량과 가격 정책에 기반한 실
적 차별화를 통해 피어 그룹 내 경쟁력을 높여갈 것으로 보인다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

목표주가 2,000,000

(26.10.02) 
1,354,000 
제약업종

◼ 3Q26 컨센서스 부합, 4Q26 성장폭 확대 전망 
◼ 5공장 Ramp-up 지속, 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용)

3Q26 Preview: 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 전망 
3Q26 매출액 1조 3,380억원(+6.4% yoy, +1.3% qoq), 영업이익 5,890억원(-
7.0% yoy, +0.4% qoq, OPM 44.0%)으로 컨센서스에 부합하는 실적을 전
망함. 3분기 평균 원/달러 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락하고, 지난 5월 
파업에 따른 생산 차질 영향이 반영되나, 5공장 PPQ 물량의 매출 인식이 
시작되며 yoy, qoq 외형 성장은 이어질 것으로 예상함.  
파업 당시 생산이 지연된 물량은 3Q 중 추가 생산되어 4Q26부터 매출에 반
영될 전망임. 5공장은 다수 프로젝트의 PPQ가 진행 중이며, Ramp-up에 
따라 매출 기여도 점진적으로 확대될 것으로 예상함. 이에 4Q26에는 파
업 이연 물량 반영과 5공장 매출 확대에 따라 실적 성장폭이 확대될 전
망임. 2026년 연간 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 5조 5,310억원(+21.4% 
yoy), 2조 4,780억원(+19.8% yoy, OPM 44.8%)으로 전망함.

신규 수주 회복 기대, 중장기 성장 기반은 확대 중 
관세 관련 불확실성으로 둔화됐던 고객사와의 수주 논의가 재개되고 있음. 
10월 2일 유럽 소재 제약사와 935억원 규모의 CMO 계약을 추가 체결
했으며, 4Q26 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목함.  
PolyPeptide 인수는 연내 거래 종결을 목표로 진행 중이며, 인수 완료 
시 펩타이드 분야로 CDMO 포트폴리오가 확대될 전망임. 6 공장도 연내 
착공을 목표로 준비 중임. 
한편, 노사 리스크는 단기 불확실성 요인임. 노조는 10/26부터 3주간 2
차 총파업을 예고함. 1차 대비 예정된 파업 기간이 길어진 만큼 실제 파
업 진행 시 생산 차질 규모가 주요 변수임. 다만 파업 개시 전까지 협상 
가능성이 열려 있어, 향후 노사 협상 진행 경과를 확인할 필요가 있음.

AUSTRALIAN DWELLING
APPROVALS BULLETIN
Units lift the headline
2 November 2026
Key points
• Total dwelling approvals rose 5.1%mth
in September, although the annual pace
moved into contraction at –0.8%yr with
the detail suggesting the underlying
cycle is starting to turn down.

• The headline increase was driven by
another volatile rise in private unit
approvals (+16.6%) which more than
offset a significant decline in private
detached house approvals (–5.3%).
• Units drove gains in NSW and
Queensland. WA, SA and Victoria all
recorded declines. Detached dwelling
approvals fell across all states.
• The total value of residential approvals
fell 2.8%mth but a sharp rise in non-
residential approvals – led by the
data centres – saw the total value of

building approvals rise 13%mth.
• Interest rate increases, weakness in
established housing markets, rising costs
and pessimistic consumer sentiment
are likely to remain headwinds.

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Total dwelling approvals rose 5.1%mth in September, while
the annual pace moved into contraction at –0.8%yr. The
result was well above both Westpac’s expectation for a
7.0%mth decline and the market expectation for a 2.0%mth
rise. Unit approvals continued to drive headline volatility,

surging in September, while detached dwellings, which
provide a better read on the underlying trend, recorded a
sizeable decline.
Note that most figures in the charts and table are presented on
a rolling-three month basis to smooth out monthly volatility.
Private detached dwelling approvals fell 5.3%mth in
September, unwinding August’s upwardly-revised 5.4%mth
rise (+1.7ppts). Recent resilience has kept the segment
elevated, with approvals remaining above 10,000 and the

annual pace at 9.6%yr. However, the quarterly profile provides
a clearer read on the underlying trend and suggests detached
dwelling approvals are beginning to respond to recent interest
rate increases (see here). Approvals rose just 0.9%qtr in
September vs 3.1%qtr in June and 5.9%qtr in March.
Private unit approvals remained volatile, rising 16.6%mth in
September following 21.7%mth decline in August. Strength
was led by high-rise approvals, while low-mid-rise approvals

recorded a more modest increase. Despite the monthly rise,
the annual pace fell further into contraction at –20.5%yr.
Among the states, NSW recorded the strongest result,
with private approvals rising 35.3%mth led by a surge in
private unit approvals. Queensland also recorded an increase
(+9.3%mth), while WA (–2.9%mth), SA (–3.5%mth) and
Victoria (–29.0%mth) all declined.
In value terms, residential building approvals fell 2.8%mth,
driven by a 3.3%mth decline in new residential approvals,

partially offset by a 0.8%mth rise in renovation activity.
Non-residential approvals once again reversed sharply, rising
45.9%mth, as the data centre-dominated ‘other commercial’
segment recorded an 8-fold increase on a non-seasonally
adjusted basis. The total value of building approvals rose
13.0%mth in September. On a non-seasonally adjusted basis,
the value of building approvals rose 8.3%mth overall, but
declined 3.0%mth when excluding the ‘other commercial’

segment.
September’s result reinforces the importance of looking
through headline volatility in unit approvals. While total
approvals rose in the month, detached dwelling approvals
slowed on a quarterly basis and fell across all states, pointing
to softer underlying momentum. The annual pace of total
approvals also moved into contraction for the first time
since January. Interest rate increases, the sharp downturn
in established housing markets and rising costs are likely to

weigh on further growth, with pessimistic consumer sentiment
an additional headwind (see here).
Detached approvals point to softer momentum

Document 313: Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-26)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-26).pdf
Report source: Merrill Lynch (CMO)
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Oil Price Dynamics Remain Key to the Outlook
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
Inflation, interest rates and equity market dynamics have changed materially since mid-
August, as widening attacks on Middle East and Russian crude oil and refining capacity
worsened supply and price conditions. Indeed, with the oil market already strained, an
early-September attack on a major Saudi Arabian export pipeline sharply raised concerns

about a protracted and deepening oil crisis. While Middle East crude exports eventually
managed to significantly rebound, increased security risks and freight costs raised
physical crude prices.
Against this backdrop, the spot Brent price surged from $92/barrel in mid-August
back into a $115 to $135/barrel price range until recently. Reflecting unusual market
tightness, other North Sea oil grades were recently trading even higher, up to $160/
barrel, according to the International Energy Agency (IEA). Even as it cooled from $135

to $115/barrel between October 2 and October 20, spot Brent is still almost double
year-ago levels, and so are many diesel prices. The AAA average U.S. retail diesel price
recently hit a new record and was 74% higher year-over-year (YoY) as of October 20.
With insufficient regional supply, the New York Harbor wholesale diesel price is 130%
higher from a year ago, according to the Energy Information Administration (EIA).
Indeed, sharply higher oil prices and a growing diesel shortage have put the global diesel

market under extreme strain. The U.S., for example, has little distillate cushion and
limited spare refining capacity. The distillate inventory (diesel and fuel oil) has decreased
from 37 days of demand coverage in January to just 26 days by early October, according
to the EIA. This 30% drop brought the supply buffer to the bottom of its historical
range. With limited refining capacity and reliance on imports for about 10% of regional
consumption, East Coast distillate inventories are the lowest since 1990, for example,

and are expected to remain below their 5-year average into late 2027, according to EIA
data. More broadly, according to the IEA’s October Oil Market Report (OMR), “oil market
balances are forecast to remain in deficit through the end of the year, with constrained
middle distillate supplies likely to persist well into 2027.”
Thus, although wage growth/labor demand do not appear inflationary, the oil crisis
risks continuing to feed “core” inflation. According to the Federal Reserve’s Beige Book 1

covering precisely the mid-August to early-October period of heightened oil-market
turmoil, “higher oil prices permeated all District reports,” with firms noting that fuel
surcharges were passing on increased costs throughout the supply chain. The 22% rise
in airfares over the past year alone contributed around 0.2 percentage point (pp) to the
2.6% 12-month increase in the “core” consumer price index (CPI) for September. Aside
from transportation costs effects, the spike in diesel prices also portends high food

prices with a few-month lag given planting/harvesting cost considerations.
By solidifying expectations for higher Fed interest rates to fight inflation, the surge in oil
prices has contributed to the spike in long-term Treasury yields this year. According to an
October 13, 2026, Empirical Research Partners report, the Iran-war oil crisis is estimated
to have contributed about 0.50 pp to the roughly 2.40 pp increase in the real U.S. long-
term Treasury rate from 2020 to early October (Exhibit 1A). According to the report, this

oil-price effect accounts for “much of the rise in real rates since February 27, 2026...”
On the positive side, while most other underpinnings for higher rates compared to the
pre-pandemic decade are structural (Exhibit 1A), the oil-price-related effect on interest
rates is reversible, implying that a resolution to the current oil crisis has the potential to
help lower both inflation and interest rates, relieving some of the current headwinds to
growth and asset prices.

Indeed, while much of the increase in interest rates since the pandemic reflects stronger,
more normal economic growth, and has thus been accompanied by surging corporate
1 Summary of Commentary on Current Economic Conditions by Federal Reserve District, Federal Reserve, October 2026.
Investment Implications
Oil market conditions remain a swing
factor for the outlook. A shift lower in
oil risk premia and improvement on
the refining front would go a long way

toward easing inflation and interest
rates while bolstering risk-asset
appetite. A gradual increase in Fixed
Income portfolio duration continues to
make sense, in our view.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
earnings and risk-asset prices, worsening oil-market conditions since mid-summer and
associated spike in yields have been accompanied by a loss of momentum in U.S. equity
prices (Exhibit 1B). Given their higher sensitivity to energy price and interest rates shocks,
Small-caps lost relatively more momentum than Large-caps during this period.
The Large-cap rally also faded, though, as oil and interest rate headwinds widened stock-

level return dispersion, with roughly 40% of constituent stocks in bear market territory
(down 20% or more). Still, buoyed by the Technology sector as well as by expectations
of conflict resolution, the market-capitalization weighted S&P 500 Index has mainly
consolidated just below its peak. While the equal-weighted S&P 500 Index stumbled more,
it was just about 3% off its peak as of October 20.
While the economy remains resilient and profit expectations remain strong, persistent

oil market turmoil makes the macro backdrop more fragile. For example, the Beige Book
not only highlights concern about spreading fuel-related inflation, as noted above, but its
language also suggests that economic activity lost some momentum since its mid-July
to late-August report. Overall, it describes growth as slight to moderate, with generally
moderate manufacturing growth except for robust activity in the energy-heavy Dallas
District. Little growth is reported in other sectors overall, with most Districts seeing little

to no change in consumer spending, for example.
Employment was described as stable to little changed, with generally adequate labor supply
except for certain specialized trade workers. Five of twelve Districts note some reports of
businesses cutting staff through attrition, delaying hiring or anticipating less job creation
as a result of AI-driven productivity gains. Most Districts reported that business maintained
a positive outlook for coming months, though “expectations tend to be for slight growth,

weighted toward manufacturing and burdened by persistent uncertainty.”
The September employment and retail sales reports seem to validate the anecdotal Beige
Book evidence. Also, the 0.4% annualized manufacturing production growth over the July to
September period (and 1.1% YoY, according to the Federal Reserve Broad), while welcome,
is hardly an indication of booming conditions. Against a backdrop of rising electricity prices,
wage growth lagging inflation, and higher gasoline prices broadly offsetting this year’s tax

cut benefits, real consumer spending is unlikely to accelerate much absent stronger labor
demand and real wage growth. A resolution to the oil crisis that brings economic relief and
tempers interest rates is thus critical for continued expansion in 2027, in our view.

Investment Implications
Portfolio maintenance is a key
component of successful investing.
It’s time for investors to assess their
portfolios—or look under the hood.
MARKET VIEW
Six Questions Investors Should Be Asking Themselves Right Now
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
After four years of rising equity prices, investors have plenty to celebrate. Despite

inflation scares, geopolitical tensions, trade wars, rising interest rates and breathless
predictions around AI, the S&P 500 has returned some 131% (total returns) since the
bull market in equities commenced in October 2022. Corporate earnings hav e generally
exceeded expectations; AI has ignited a historic investment boom, and America’s
technology giants have powered the market to new heights.
We believe the bull will rumble on into 2027. We also believe that now is the time for

investors to take stock of their portfolios, since bull markets have a way of changing the
composition of portfolios. In addition, investors should not assume that the investment
strategies that worked well in the past four years will necessarily deliver comparable
results during the next four. Below are six questions investors should be asking
themselves right now:
Number One: How concentrated is my portfolio? Many investors believe they are
invested in the stock market when, in fact, they are invested in a handful of companies—

specifically AI-related names that have soared and dominated the index over the past
few years. Information technology now accounts for roughly 40% of the market cap of
the S&P 500, while the top 10 companies represent a share of 38.4%.
As the Financial Times recently noted, U.S. equity and credit markets have become so
increasingly concentrated in AI-related groups that “more than tw o-thirds of companies
in the Russell 1000 index of large U.S. groups are linked to AI, either directly or

indirectly or because the technology is becoming embedded in their business models.” 2
Meanwhile, Big Technology hyperscalers and companies that are beneficiaries of the AI
spending boom make up about 16% of the U.S. high-grade bond market after a huge
borrowing binge, making the grouping the largest in the investment-grade bond market. 2
The upshot: Investors that have been rewarded by concentration need to ask themselves
what percentage of their wealth is tied to the all-encompassing AI trade and consider

what would happen if mega-cap tech leadership falters.
Number Two: How diversified is my portfolio? While concentration asks whether
investors own too much of a few assets, diversification asks whether investors own enough
of different assets that behave differently when economic cycles or financial conditions
change. A portfolio containing hundreds of U.S. Equities via exchange-traded funds, mutual
funds and other assets may appear diversified. But if those companies are overwhelmingly

exposed to the same economic cycle, interest rate environment, or technology theme,
the diversification benefits are diluted. Case in point: While we remain constructive on
Emerging Market (EM) Equities, investors need to understand that a significant driver of
EM returns has come from AI-driven markets like Taiwan and South Korea.
Today, true diversification means considering exposure to hard assets (commodities, real
estate), EM mining and agricultural leaders, EM bonds and currencies, China bio-technology,

Small-cap international, and Private Equity and Hedge Funds for qualified investors.
Number Three: How tax-efficient is my portfolio? Bull markets create wealth—in
addition to tax liabilities. The good news is that after four years of rising equity returns,
investors are sitting on sizable, unrealized capital gains. The bad news: Market gains
can feel like a financial straitjacket, making it difficult to rebalance portfolios without
triggering significant tax liabilities.

Against this backdrop, now is the time for investors to review where the assets are
held—distinguishing between taxable and tax-advantaged accounts, and the attendant tax
ramifications. The appropriate mix depends on individual circumstances, including future
2 “Investors seek shelter from heightened exposure to AI”, The Financial Times, October 6, 2026.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
withdrawal taxes. It is also time to consider tax-loss strategies, charitable donations of
securities and other tactics and strategies since in the end, the measure of inv estment
success isn’t simply how much money investors make. It’s how much they keep.
Number Four: Is it time to take profits?* There is a peculiarity to bull markets: The
more money investors make, the more the attendant tax liabilities. Tax liabilities, in turn,

can discourage selling, and the reluctance to sell can leave investors dangerously exposed
to yesterday’s leaders and compound concentration risks. That brings up an emotionally
difficult decision: When should investors sell their leaders or take some chips off the table?
The question is notably relevant today, given four years of substantial Equity gains.
Beyond tax considerations, investors need to understand that profit-taking can create
financial flexibility. Realized after-tax gains can replenish cash reserves, fund near-

term spending needs, reduce leverage or most importantly, provide capital to rebalance
portfolios in other areas of the market. In general, the goal of taking profits isn’t to get
out of the market—it is to ensure that after years of accumulated gains, these proceeds
are redeployed and redistributed across various asset classes that continue to support
and underpin long-term wealth accumulation.
Number Five: How well is my portfolio positioned for a new interest rate

regime? For much of the past quarter century, investors operated in a world of copious
liquidity, declining interest rates and relatively cheap capital. The era of capital abundance
favored stocks over bonds, but that era is over—we are shifting to a world of capital
scarcity, or intense competition for capital and the attendant hike in the cost of capital.
In this world, higher rates weigh the most on long-duration assets. Positioning for this
regime favors companies with strong balance sheets and cash flow over firms with heavy

debt or refinancing needs. High-quality bonds are more attractiv e in this environment;
sovereign debt risks are concentrated more overseas (i.e., France) than the U.S.
Number Six: How well is my portfolio positioned for the next shock? Shocks
happen. If the past four years have taught investors anything, it is that the unexpected
should be the expected. This decade alone has produced gut-wrenching market mo ves
related to a global pandemic, a ground war in Europe, the reconfiguration of global

supply chains, epic technological change and a war in the Middle East. Through it all, U.S.
Equities have trended higher but hardly in a linear fashion.
The next shocks could emerge in the global bond market, where mounting go vernment
debt and higher borrowing costs have triggered mounting volatility. It could emerge from
disappointing returns on massive AI investments. Or it could come from a geopolitical
event, another energy shock or any other unforeseen event. Investors can’t predict the

next shock, but they can prepare for it. So now is the time for investors to stress test
their portfolios against a potential shock, examining how a steep downdraft in Equities
or spike in bond yields would affect their retirement plans, liquidity needs and other
financial goals and objectives.
Exhibit 2: Six Questions to Stress Test Your Portfolio.
The six questions What investors should examine Portfolio diagnostic
1. How concentrated am I? Mega-cap technology exposure and overlapping holdings % of portfolio in top 10 stocks

2. Am I truly diversified? Asset allocation, geographic mix and correlated exposures Current equity/bond mix versus target
3. How tax-efficient am I? Unrealized gains, asset location and tax-loss harvesting Embedded capital gains and estimated tax cost of rebalancing
4. Is it time to take profits? Portfolio drift, oversized winners and valuations Positions exceeding target allocation
5. Am I ready for higher interest rates? Duration, leverage, refinancing risks and cash flows Bond duration and exposure to highly leveraged companies

6. Am I prepared for the next shock? Liquidity, downside exposure and portfolio resilience Estimated loss in a 20% equity selloff
Source: Chief Investment Office. Data as of October 22, 2026.
* “Pop the Champagne,” Portfolio Insights, Chief Investment Office, August 2026.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What are rising credit spreads signaling about the broader market environment?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Officer
Rising French bond yields with a sharp rise in spreads above German bunds, mixed
with AI buildout concerns, are creating nervousness across the capital markets. This
nervousness centers around the ability to fund deficits, the potential for slower growth

and the future return on the announced capital investment. We expect these concerns
to continue to linger well into next year as growth worries remain in France, bond
yields stay sticky around current levels, and, in the case of AI, investors demand more
compensation for financing.
At this stage, we do not expect the rising spreads in targeted areas to be a precursor
for a material slowdown in economic growth overall. Rather, we believe this is a natural

signal that investors are moving up the quality stack as we move into the middle of
the AI buildout. The bottom line is that rising credit spreads in lower quality corners of
the market are natural along the lifecycle of financing. When free cash flow is called
into question, or when debt financing costs begin to approach the expected return on
investment, investors begin to reprice risk.
We continue to emphasize higher quality balance sheets in corporate and sovereign

credit as well as higher quality leaders in the AI buildout overall. The closer we get to
2027, investors will likely begin to transition from too much exposure to the “buildout”
and not enough exposure to “AI adopters.”

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
SS&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and
services.
S&P 500 Market-cap Weighted Index is a float-adjusted market-capitalization-weighted index, meaning a company's influence and percentage weight in the index are determined by its
total market value (share price multiplied by the number of outstanding shares).

S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is a variation of the traditional S&P 500 where every single company in the index is given the exact same weight—0.2%—regardless of its size or market
capitalization.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Industrials Index imposes equal weights on the index constituents included in the S&P 500 that are classified in the GICS® industrials sector.
Russell 1000 Index is a stock market index that tracks the performance of the 1,000 largest publicly traded companies in the United States.

Bank of America, Merrill, their affiliates and advisors do not provide legal, tax or accounting advice. Clients should consult their legal and/or tax advisors before making any financial decisions.
This material does not take into account a client’s particular investment objectives, financial situations, or needs and is not intended as a recommendation, offer, or solicitation for the purchase
or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.
The Chief Investment Office (“CIO”) provides thought leadership on wealth management, investment strategy and global markets; portfolio management solutions; due diligence; and solutions
oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.
BofA Global Research is research produced by BofA Securities, Inc. (“BofAS”) and/or one or more of its affiliates. BofAS is a registered broker-dealer, Member SIPC and wholly owned subsidiary

of Bank of America Corporation.
All recommendations must be considered in the context of an individual investor’s goals, time horizon, liquidity needs and risk tolerance. Not all recommendations will be in the best interest
of all investors.
Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.
Nonfinancial assets, such as closely held businesses, real estate, fine art, oil, gas and mineral properties, and timber, farm and ranch land, are complex in nature and involve risks including total

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025.

lẻ/ngày theo quý

### Page 2

2

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T 
 — PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ.

Phương án bổ sung vốn và tái cơ cấu hoạt động kinh doanh 
— Đại hội thông qua phương án chào bán tối đa 550 triệu cổ phiếu. Nguồn vốn dự kiến 
phân bổ 60% cho kế hoạch kinh doanh gắn với điều chỉnh chính sách thu đổi, 20% bổ 
sung vốn lưu động và phần còn lại bổ sung vốn lưu động và/hoặc trả vay ngắn hạn. 
Nhu cầu vốn Q4/2026 và Q1/2027 lần lượt khoảng 3,100 tỷ VND và 2,800 tỷ VND. Giá và 
khối lượng phát hành thực tế chưa được xác định tại đại hội.  
— Doanh nghiệp dự kiến đóng 25–30 cửa hàng và mở khoảng 10 cửa hàng (có chọn lọc) 
trong năm 2026, tương ứng giảm ròng 15–20 cửa hàng; đồng thời đặt mục tiêu giảm 
20–30% chi phí nhân sự. Cổ tức tiền mặt năm 2026 được điều chỉnh về 0%; cổ tức 2025 
về 10% mệnh giá và đã chi trả. 
— 100% mẫu kim cương được AIGS và Vinacontrol kiểm định độc lập là kim cương thiên 
nhiên, phù hợp thông số 4C. PNJ dự kiến trang bị máy kiểm định tại các cửa hàng trọng 
điểm để khách hàng kiểm tra trực tiếp.

Đánh giá của KBSV 
— KBSV cho rằng huy động vốn từ phát hành riêng lẻ là phương án phù hợp ở thời điểm 
hiện tại trong bối cảnh lãi suất tăng cao, giúp PNJ cân đối thanh khoản và duy trì hoạt 
động liên tục. Tuy nhiên, với khối lượng phát hành lớn và chưa có phương án chi tiết, 
doanh nghiệp sẽ cần thêm thời gian để hoàn thành phương án phát hành và cân đối 
giữa lợi ích của cổ đông và doanh nghiệp.  
— Nhu cầu yếu tại Trung Quốc và cạnh tranh từ kim cương nuôi cấy giá thấp tiếp tục gây 
áp lực lên giá kim cương tự nhiên, khiến PNJ gặp khó khăn trong việc tiêu thụ tồn kho 
nhóm kim cương  và gia tăng tổn thất khi mua lại theo chính sách cũ. Thực tế, tỷ trọng 
kim cương và trang sức kim cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% cuối Q2/2026 lên 
52% cuối Q3/2026, với giá trị ước khoảng 9,000 tỷ VND. KBSV cho rằng cần theo dõi (1) 
nhu cầu thị trường trong giai đoạn cuối năm; (2) tốc độ giảm tồn kho và dòng tiền thu 
về từ bán hàng; và (3) giá trị khách hàng bán lại. Diễn biến các yếu tố này sẽ quyết định 
khả năng thu hồi vốn, mức độ phục hồi biên lợi nhuận và nhu cầu trích lập bổ sung 
trong quá trình xử lý. 
— Ước tính bán niên sử dụng tỷ lệ tổn thất 20.3% đối với hàng bán trong giai đoạn 2020–
2026 dự kiến được thu mua lại, thấp hơn mức 28.9% trên các giao dịch mua lại thực tế 
từ 01/07–03/09/2026. Vì vậy, bên cạnh giả định thu đổi 80%, mức tổn thất khi xử lý 
hàng mua lại là yếu tố quan trọng cần theo dõi. Việc chuyển hạch toán dự phòng từ 
SG&A sang giá vốn tại kỳ soát xét bán niên cũng cho thấy áp lực biên lợi nhuận bị co 
hẹp trong quá trình tái cấu trúc về mặt kế toán.

動態更新  硬體‧美國

Flex 
(FLEX.O/ FLEX US)

預期資料中心電源需求為獲利加速成長之主要動能 
重要訊息 
Flex FY2Q27 財報與 FY3Q27 財測均優於市場共識 ，公司上修 FY2027 資料
中心年增率自 70%至 80%，收盤股價上漲約 12%。 
評論及分析  
FY2Q27 資料中心電源需求升溫， EPS 年增 49%至 1.18 美元並優於共識。
Flex FY2Q27 (2026 年 7–9 月)營收年增 25%、季增 7%至 84.9 億美元，主
要來自資料中心電源 需求延續 ；受惠於電源產品營利率 年增 5.5ppt s 至約
16.0%、帶動產品組合轉佳， Flex FY2Q27  EPS 年增 49%、季增 18%至
1.18 美元，大致符合凱基原預估、優於市場共識 12%。 
FY3Q27 資料中心成長性可望優於 FY2Q27，預估 EPS 年增 52%至 1.32 美
元。Flex 已於 10 月完成對 EPC Power 的收購，該公司可望貢獻單季營收約
2 億美元，並有助於 800V 架構及固態變壓器( SST)的技術能力。FY 3Q27 隨
Hyperscalers 與 Neo clouds 等客戶需求延續，凱基預估 Flex 營收年增
33%、季增 11%至 93.9 億美元，其中資料中心業務之營收年增率預期達
115%，其餘產品線則約 10%上下；受惠營利率較佳的資料中心業務貢獻提
升，FY3Q27  EPS 預估年增 52%、季增 11%至 1.32 美元。 
凱基預估 Flex FY2027/FY2028  EPS 為 5.24/8.80 美元。基於電源產品接單
成長性優於預期以及併購 EPC Power 也將挹注營收，Flex 上修 FY2027 資料
中心營收年增率 自 70%至 80%，FY2028 則維持年增 80%之展望，隱含
FY2027/FY2028 資料中心營收分別上修 約 6% 、營收占比預期達
33%/45%。基於 Flex 上修展望，凱基 將 Flex FY2027/ FY2028 EPS 自原預估
之 4.96/7.96 美元上修至 5.24/8.80 美元，分別年增 59%/68%。 
投資建議  
凱基維持「增加持股」評等，目標價上修至 176 美元 (20x FY2028F EPS) 。 
投資風險  
關稅戰惡化導致客戶下單意願降低； CPI 業務營運成長態勢不如預期。

### Page 2

美國 Flex

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  2

Flex 資料中心營收占比提升至 30%。Flex 三大產品線為 ITS (包含網通與智慧
生活，FY 2Q27 營收占比 39%；營利率 5.5%)、RMS ( 包含工業、汽車與醫
療，FY1Q27 營收占比 31%；營利率 6.4%)、CPI ( 資料中心業務，FY1Q27
營收占比 30%；營利率 10.7%)。各項業務之應用主要包含:  (1) 工業產品涵
蓋資本設備、工業裝置、再生能源、電源與電網邊緣等解決方案； (2) 車用
產品主要提供下一代移動方案、自駕、連網化 、電動化與智慧科技 ；(3) 健
康解決方案包含醫療裝置、醫療設備與藥物輸送系統； (4) 智慧生活包含消
費性電子、家電、消費性包裝、掃地機器人、微型移動裝置及音訊產品； (5) 
網通領域涵蓋通訊基礎設施與企業網路傳輸； (6) 資料中心業務 包含電源、
散熱、系統整合等產品與服務。   
Flex 目前的發展策略是結合 EMS 與產品的服務策略以提升接單能力，公司
表示自身是全球唯一具有提供從資料中心晶片到電網能力的供應商，除了可
提供嵌入式電源 (embedded power) 產品、管理伺服器 GPU 的電源，亦可
滿足客戶對資料中心內所有設施的電力與散熱需求，提供完整的客製化服
務，帶動公司資料中心營收占比自前一季之 28%提升至 FY2Q27 之 30%。

圖 1: Flex CPI部門為近幾季主要成長動能，營收占比逐步提升至30%

Flex FY2027/ FY2028 資料中心營收與獲利成長性可望優於產業平均 。Flex
計劃將 CPI 部門分拆成一家新的上市公司 Axiom，維持在 CY1Q27 完成分拆
的進度不變， Flex 自身將專注於醫療保健、機器人、自動化倉儲和網通等產
業，兩家公司可更聚焦 於彼此的業務並提升資金運用效率。公司 提及自身在
資料中心電源布局相對完整，且產品價格亦相對具有競爭優勢下，使公司在
電源產品接單成長性優於產業平均； 隨包含 Google 在內的 Hyperscalers 、
Colocations 及 Neo-clouds 等客戶對嵌入式電源與 系統級解決方案的需求升
溫，公司在此次會議將 FY2027 資料中心營收年增率自 70%上修至 80%、
FY2028 則維持年增 80%之展望，皆較 FY2026 資料中心業務營收年增 38%
至 66 億美元加速成長 ，並隱含 FY2027/FY2028 資料中心營收分別上 修約
6%、營收占比預期達 33%/45%。

### Page 3

美國 Flex

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  3

獲利性部分， 受惠於電源產品營利率較佳、 FY2Q27 年增 5.5ppts 至約
16.0%，優於資料中心業務的平均營利率約 11%以及公司整體平均營利率
約 7%，公司上修 FY2027 資料中心業務之營利率約 1ppt 至 11.6%。凱基
預估隨資料中心營收貢獻提升， FY2027/FY2028 營 利占比分別達
49%/65%，為公司主要獲利動能。  
至於資料中心以外之 其餘應用，預期營收年增率 維持約 10%上下之水準。
目前歐洲工業需求略 有回溫，營收年增率可望維持約 10%上下；汽車產品
線則因目前全球汽車銷售量已逐步回升至過往 9,000 萬輛的正常水準，
FY2027 /FY2028 產品線成長性預期落在 5%以內；醫療設備則因客戶先前在
Covid -19 時期堆積的庫存水位過高，目前庫存去化雖逐步落底，但 需求端仍
相對平緩；網通領域則預期隨庫存去化力道放緩， FY2027 產品線營收將逐
步回溫，預期年增率 約 10%；消費性電子則因記憶體漲價、品牌廠將成本
轉嫁給消費者的影響， FY202 7 營收預期年減約 5-10%。 
Flex 評等維持「增加持股」，目標價上修至 176 美元。基於 Flex 上修資料
中心之營收與營 利率展望，凱基將 Flex FY2027/FY2028 EPS 自原預估之
4.96/7.96 美元上修至 5.24/8.80 美元，分別年增 59%/68%；相較市場共識
FY202 7/FY2028  EPS 預估為 4.48/6.82 美元，股價交易於約 12/14 倍之
FY2028 凱基與市場共識 EPS 預估。 
Flex 過往五年本益比多落於 5-20 倍之間，考量 (1) 公司在資料中心領域掌握
從晶片、液冷散熱、BBU 到電網的供應能力 ，布局相對多數同業更全面 ；(2) 
公司持續提升在 CSP 廠供應鏈之接單量，有助 FY2027 /FY2028 營收與獲利
加速成長； (3) 基於資料中心產品線營利率可望逐步提升至 10-15%、顯著
優於公司過往產品之 5%水準，且在獲利占比逐步提升至 50%以上的帶動
下，有助公司評價向散熱與電源同業靠攏。綜合上述 原因，評估 Flex 本益比
可望回升至區間 上緣之 20 倍上下浮動 ；基於 Flex 當前評價偏低 ，故維持
「增加持股」之投資評等，目標價隨 EPS 預估上修而調整至 176 美元 (20x 
FY202 8F EPS)。

### Page 7

美國 Flex

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 7

項目 定義 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公用
事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下 
購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源 
再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例 
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內 
廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物 
總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮 
環保支出 環保支出總金額 - 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處置、處理、衛生、清理支出 
員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減
或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數 
女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100 
女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100 
教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作 
每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數 
股東治理分數 衡量公司用以反收購工具的有效性 
公司治理分數 衡量公司對最佳治理原則的承諾和有效性 
產品責任分數 衡量公司生產優質產品和服務的能力，且產品是否將客戶的健康、安全、整合性和數據隱私進行綜合考量 
社區關係分數 衡量公司對成為優良公民、保護公眾健康和尊重商業道德的承諾 
勞動力分數 衡量公司在員工工作滿意度、健康、工作場所的安全、多樣性、平等以及員工發展機會方面的成效 
資源使用指標 衡量公司在原物料、能源或水的使用效率，以及是否通過改進供應鏈來尋求更俱生態效率的解決方案

Document 349: European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30)

Source PDF: all report pdf/European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

European resilience, American vigour

Eurozone growth continues to prove resilient, notwithstanding the volatility of the Irish figures. The Q3 data reinforce 
our forecast of 1.1% growth this year, which could even be slightly exceeded, as German and Spanish momentum shows 
no sign of fading. In France, the negative surprise (-0.1% q/q against a consensus of +0.1% q/q) stems from a new one-
off factor (the sharp fall in aerospace output caused by delays in input deliveries) and masks an encouraging rebound 
in household consumption. In the United  States, growth reached its highest level since Q2 2025 (0.8% q/q), driven by 
buoyant private domestic demand.

Eurozone: The resilience of growth is confirmed 
Eurozone GDP grew by 0.4% q/q in Q3  according to Eurostat's preliminary flash estimate, above both the consensus and our 
Nowcast (0.3%). The ever -volatile Irish figures contributed 0.2 pp to this outcome. Excluding Ireland, growth (0.2% q/q) 
nonetheless remains in line with the pace of recent quarters (0.2 -0.3% q/q on average). This illustrates the Eurozone's capacity 
to withstand the current shocks, thanks in particular to the strength of Spain (+0.07 pp contribution to the q/q change in Eurozone’s 
GDP) and the Netherlands (+0.04 pp), and to Germany's gradual recovery (+0.06 pp). France (-0.03 pp) and Italy (zero growth), 
by contrast, posted disappointing results, although in France's case these partly reflect temporary factors (aerospace output). The 
growth carry-over for 2026 now stands at 1.0%, which supports our forecast of 1.1% in annual average terms and even points to 
upside risk. The European Commission's October surveys remain upbeat, with a further improvement in industrial activity 
expectations. 
The German recovery continues . As anticipated by our  Nowcast, real GDP grew by 0.2% q/q in Q3 (after 0.3% in Q2).  
According to Destatis, consumption (private and public) increased slightly, while investment edged down. Export momentum 
slowed markedly compared with Q2. This is Germany's fourth consecutive quarter of growth. 
Italian GDP surprised on the downside, stagnating in Q3 (against a consensus of 0.1%).  According to Istat, this reflects a 
decline in value added in industry, offset by increases in services and agriculture. The positive contribution from domestic demand 
was reportedly cancelled out by a negative contribution from net trade. 
Spanish growth remained robust in Q3, in line with expectations (0.6% q/q, after 0.7% in Q2),  driven mainly by domestic 
demand (0.5 pp contribution) and, to a lesser extent, by net exports (0.1 pp). Consumption remains the main engine of growth 
(+0.6% q/q for private consumption and +0.9% for public consumption). Investment rose by 0.7%. Export gr owth (1.3%) once 
again outpaced import growth (1.1%) this quarter. All sectors except construction recorded positive changes in value added, with 
industry and services both up 0.7%. 
France: A sharp drop in aerospace output prevents a return to growth in Q3 
GDP contracted by 0.1% q/q in Q3 (after stagnating in Q2), weighed down by a sharp fall in aerospace output (-4.5% q/q 
in the production of other transport equipment, which includes aerospace). This steep decline dragged down goods production 
(down 1.2% q/q) and explains the sharp contraction in exports ( -1.7% q/q) and business investment (-0.4% q/q). Net trade thus 
subtracted 0.6 pp from GDP (imports were flat over the same period). Conversely, household consumption surprised on the 
upside (+0.4% q/q), boosted by purchases of electric vehicles (registrations up 112% y/y in Q3). Public consumption 
(+0.3% q/q) also supported growth, as did activity in market services (+0.3% q/q).  Despite the sharp drop in aerospace 
output in Q3, Airbus has confirmed its delivery targets for the year (with engine supply problems expected to be resolved), pointing 
to a strong rebound in growth in Q4 (0.3% q/q according to our forecasts). Overall, gr owth is expected to reach 0.3% in 2026 , 
before picking up to 0.8% in 2027. 
United States: Growth at a one-year high  
GDP growth picked up in Q3 to 3.4% at a seasonally adjusted annualised rate ( AR; i.e. 0.8% q/q), from 2.2% in Q2 
(0.6% q/q). This is the fastest pace since Q3 2025, confirming that activity is almost immune to the energy shock. Private domestic 
demand remains particularly strong. Private non-residential investment accelerated to 12.7% AR, a performance unmatched since 
the post-Covid reopening. AI-related components are driving this figure (18.5% AR), but other types of investment are also rising 
for a second consecutive quarter (3.8% AR) after contracting uninterruptedly throughout 2025. Household consumption, steady 
at 2.5% AR in real terms, is growing faster than it did before inflation picked up again, but at the cost of a significant drop in the 
saving rate (3.8% at the end of the quarter, down from 4.4% at the end of the previous quarter). The United States nonetheles s 
continues to "import its growth": net trade is weighing on GDP owing to a surge in imports (19.7% AR), driven by AI -related 
components – IT, semiconductors and telecommunications (103.8% AR).

AGGREGATE ESTIMATES AND REVISIONS 
 26Q3 Y/Y earnings are expected to be 31.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the Y/Y earnings estimate is 26.4%. 
 Of the 299 companies in the S&P 500 that have reported earnings to date for 26Q3, 81.6% have reported earnings 
above analyst estimates. This compares to a long-term average of 67.7% and prior four quarter average of 81.5%. 
 26Q3 Y/Y revenue is expected to be 13.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the growth estimate is 12.2%. 
 69.9% of companies have reported 26Q3 revenue above analyst expectations. This compares to a long-term 
average of 63% and an average over the past four quarters of 76.8%. 
 For 26Q3, there have been 40 negative EPS preannouncements issued by S&P 500 corporations compared to 66 
positive EPS preannouncements. By dividing 40 by 66 the N/P ratio is 0.6 for the S&P 500 Index.  
 The forward four-quarter (26Q4– 27Q3) P/E ratio for the S&P 500 is 19.0. 
 During the week of Nov. 2, 144 S&P 500 companies are expected to report quarterly earnings.

decreases in earnings, while the energy (15.7B) sector has recorded the highest dollar-level increase in earnings.

The estimated earnings growth rate for the S&P 500 for 26Q3 is 31.6%. If the energy sector is excluded, the growth 
rate declines to 26.4%. The S&P 500 expects to see share-weighted earnings of $778.4B in 26Q3, compared to share-
weighted earnings of $591.4B (based on the year-ago earnings of the current 503 constituents) in 25Q3. 
11 of the 11 sectors in the index expect to see an improvement in earnings relative to 25Q3. The Energy and 
Information Technology sectors have the highest earnings growth rates for the quarter, while the Consumer 
Discretionary sector has the weakest anticipated growth compared to 25Q3. 
The Energy sector has the highest earnings growth rate (146.2%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $63B in 26Q3, 
compared to earnings of $25.6B in 25Q3. 4 of the 5 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see higher earnings 
than a year ago. The Oil & Gas Refining & Marketing (566.5%) and Integrated Oil & Gas (143.8%) sub-industries have 
the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate declines to 39%. 
The Information Technology sector has the second highest earnings growth rate (68.9%) of any sector. It is expected 
to earn $260.2B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $154B in 25Q3. 12 of the 12 sub-industries in the sector are 
anticipated to see higher earnings than a year ago. The Semiconductors (137%) and Semiconductor Materials & 
Equipment (71.5%) sub-industries have the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, 
the growth rate declines to 28.2%. 
The Consumer Discretionary sector has the lowest earnings growth rate (3.9%) of any sector. It is expected to earn 
$56.3B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $54.2B in 25Q3. 7 of the 18 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to 
see lower earnings than a year ago. The Casinos & Gaming (-44.1%) and Automotive Parts & Equipment (-38.8%) 
sub-industries have the lowest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate 
improves to 4.5%.
